# Link Libraries: Entry Points

Generated by `scripts/brf_library_catalog.py`. Do not edit by hand.

Every `:BRF` file on the Norsk Data Software Archive floppies, with every name it defines.
This is what `LOAD` in the BRF linker can resolve. To see which libraries define a name, see [LINK-LIBRARY-NAME-INDEX.md](LINK-LIBRARY-NAME-INDEX.md).

**How it was made.** 1102 floppy images were read; 898 are NDFS volumes (the others are not). 585 `:BRF` files were found, 378 with different contents. Each was parsed by [nd100_brf.py](../../scripts/nd100_brf.py), which checks every unit's checksum. The parser was compared with the BRF linker's own `LIST-BRF-ENTRIES` on `FORT48-2BANK-F02:BRF`: 462 of 462 names identical.

**Library or not.** A *library file* has units that start with a LIBR group: the linker loads such a unit only when something refers to one of its names. 214 of the files are libraries (33325 names in them). The other 164 are ordinary object files and are listed at the end by name only.

**Checksum column.** `ok` means every unit's checksum is correct. `N of M units fail` means the file is damaged, or holds a group this parser reads wrongly. A copy with failures often sits next to a good copy of the same file (compare the sizes and the volumes).

**Using it.** The six-bit symbol names are at most 7 characters. When the linker says `Undefined entries`, look the name up in the index, then `LOAD` the library that defines it.
